38 Evaṃ vutte Rāsiyo gāmaṇi Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||  
Abhikkantam bhante || pe ||  
upāsakam mam Bhagavā dhāretu ajjatagge pāṇupetaṃ saraṇaṃ gatan ti || ||  

SN_4,42(8).13 Pātali (or Manāpo) 1 Ekam samayam Bhagavā Koḷiyesu viharati Uttaram[^340-2] nāma Koḷiyānaṃ nigame[^340-3] || ||  
2 Atha kho Pāṭaliyo gāmaṇi yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || ||  
Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho Pāṭaliyo gāmaṇi Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||  
Sutam me tam bhante Samaṇo Gotamo māyaṃ jānātīti || ||  
Ye te bhante evam ahaṃsu Samaṇo Gotamo māyaṃ jānātīti ||  
kacci te bhante Bhagavato vuttavādino na ca Bhagavantam abhūtena abbhācikkhanti ||  
dhammassa cānudhammaṃ vyākaronti ||  
na ca koci sahadhammiko vādānupāto[^340-4] gārayhaṃ ṭhānam āgacchati ||  
anabbhakkhātukāmā hi mayam bhante Bhagavantanti || ||  
3 Ye te gāmaṇi evam āhaṃsu Samaṇo Gotamo māyaṃ jānātīti vuttavādino ceva me te na ca mam abhūtena abbhācikkhanti ||  
dhammassa cānudhammaṃ vyākaronti ||  
na ca koci sahadhammiko vādānupāto gārayhaṃ ṭhānam āgacchatīti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 341 -->
<a id="page-341"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 341]*

4 Saccaṃ yeva kira bho mayam tesaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ na saddahāma Samaṇo Gotamo māyaṃ jānātīti ||  
Samaṇo khalu bho Gotamo māyāvīti || ||  
Yo nu kho gāmaṇi evam vadeti Aham māyaṃ jānāmīti so evaṃ vadeti Aham māyāvīti ||  
tatheva tam Bhagavā hoti tatheva tam sugato hotīti || ||  
Tena hi gāmaṇi taññevettha paṭipucchissāmi ||  
yathā te khameyya tathā taṃ vyākareyyāsi || ||  

I (I) 5 Taṃ kim maññasi gāmaṇi ||[^341-1]  
jānāsi tvaṃ gāmaṇi[^341-2] Koḷiyānaṃ lambacūḷake bhaṭe[^341-3] ti || ||  
Jānāmaham bhante Koḷiyānaṃ lambacūlake bhaṭe ti || ||  
6 Taṃ kim maññasi gāmaṇi ||  
kim atthiyā Koliyānaṃ lambacūlakā bhaṭāti || ||  
Ye ca bhante Koḷiyānaṃ corā te ca paṭisedhetuṃ yāni ca Koḷiyānam duteyyāni tāni vahātuṃ[^341-4] etadatthiyā[^341-5] bhante Koḷiyānaṃ lambacūḷakā bhaṭāti[^341-6] ||  
7 Taṃ kim maññasi gāmaṇi ||  
jānāsi tvaṃ Koḷiyānaṃ[^341-7] lambacūḷake bhaṭe ||  
silavanto vā te dussīlā[^341-8] vā ti || ||  
Jānāmaham bhante Koḷiyānaṃ lambacūlake bhaṭe dussīle pāpadhamme ||  
ye ca loke dussilā pāpadhammā Koḷiyānaṃ lambacūḷakā tesam aññatarāti || ||  
8 Yo nu kho gāmaṇi evam vadeyya || ||  
Pātaliyo gāmaṇi jānāti Koḷiyānam lambacūḷake bhaṭe dussīle pāpadhamme ||  
Pāṭaliyo pi gāmaṇi dussīlo pāpadhammoti ||  
sammā nu kho so vadamāno vadeyya || ||  
No hetam bhante ||  
aññe bhante Koḷiyānaṃ lambacūḷakā bhaṭā añño hamasmi aññathādhammā Koḷiyānam lambacūḷakā bhaṭā aññathādhammo hamasmīti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 342 -->
<a id="page-342"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 342]*

9 Tvaṃ hi nāma[^342-1] gāmaṇi[^342-2] lacchasi Pāṭaliyo gāmaṇi jānāti Koḷiyānaṃ lambacūḷake bhaṭe dussīle pāpadhamme na ca[^342-3] Pāṭaliyo gāmaṇi dussīlo pāpadhammoti ||  
kasmā tathāgato na lacchati Tathāgato māyaṃ jānāti na ca tathāgato māyāvīti || ||  
Māyañcāhaṃ gāmaṇi pajānāmi ||  
māyāya ca vipākaṃ ||  
yathāpaṭipanno ca māyāvī kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayam upapajjati tañca pajānāmi || ||  

(II) 10 Paṇātipātañcāhaṃ gāmaṇi pajānāmi pāṇātipātassa ca vipākaṃ ||  
yathāpaṭipanno ca pāṇātipātā kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayam upapajjati tañca pajānāmi ||  
11 Adinnādānañcāham gāmaṇi pajānāmi ||  
adinnādānassa ca vipākaṃ ||  
yathāpaṭipanno ca adinnādāyī kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayam upapajjati tañca pajānāmi || ||  
12 Kāmesu micchācārañ cāhaṃ gāmaṇi pajānāmi kāmesu micchācārassa ca vipākaṃ ||  
yathāpaṭipanno ca kāmesu micchācārī kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātam nirayam upapajjati tañca pajānāmi || ||  
13 Musāvādañcāham gāmaṇi pajānāmi ||  
musāvādassa ca vipākaṃ ||  
yathāpaṭipanno ca musāvādī kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatim vinipātaṃ nirayam upapajjati tañca pajānāmi || ||  
14 Pisuṇavācañcāhaṃ gāmaṇi pijānāmi pasuṇavācāya[^342-4] ca[^342-2] vipākaṃ ||  
yathāpaṭipanno ca pisunavāco[^342-5] kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayam upapajjati tañca pajānāmi ||  
15 Pharusavācañcāhaṃ gāmaṇi pajānāmi pharusavācāya[^342-6] ca vipākaṃ ||  
yathāpaṭipanno ca pharusavāco[^342-6] kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatim vinipātaṃ nirayam upapajjati tañca pajānāmi || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 343 -->
<a id="page-343"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 343]*

16 Samphappalāpañcāhaṃ gāmaṇi pajānāmi samphappalāpassa ca vipākam ||  
yathāpaṭipanno ca samphappalāpī kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayam upapajjati tañca pajānāmi || ||  
17 Abhijjhañcāhaṃ gāmaṇi pajānāmi abhijjhāya ca vipākaṃ ||  
yathāpaṭipanno ca abhijjhālu kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayam upapajjati tañca pajānāmi || ||  
18 Vyāpādapadosañcāham gāmani pajānāmi vyāpādapadosassa ca vipākaṃ ||  
yathāpaṭipanno ca vyāpannacitto kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātam nirayam upapajjati tañca pajānāmi || ||  
19 Micchādiṭṭhiñcāham gāmaṇi pajānāmi micchādiṭṭhiyā ca vipākaṃ ||  
yathāpaṭipanno ca micchādiṭṭhiko kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayam upapajjati tañca pajānāmi || ||  

II 20 Santi[^343-1] gāmaṇi eke samaṇabrāhmaṇā evaṃvādino evam diṭṭhino Yo koci pāṇam atimāpeti sabbo so diṭṭheva dhamme dukkhaṃ domanassam {paṭisaṃvediyati} ||  
yo koci adinnam ādiyati sabbo so diṭṭheva dhamme dukkhaṃ domanassam {paṭisaṃvediyati} ||  
yo koci kāmesu micchācarati sabbo so diṭṭheva dhamme dukkhaṃ domanassam {paṭisaṃvediyati} ||  
yo koci musā bhaṇati sabbo so diṭṭheva dhamme dukkhaṃ domanassam paṭisaṃvediyatīti || ||  

(I) 21 Dissati kho pana gāmani idhekacco mālī kuṇḍalī[^343-2] sunhāto[^343-3] suvilitto kappitakesamassu itthikamehi[^343-4] rājāmaññe paricārento ||  
tam[^343-5] ekam āhaṃsu ||  
Ambho ayam puriso kim akāsi mālī kuṇḍalī[^343-6] sunhāto suvilitto kappitakesamassu itthikāmehi[^343-4] rājāmaññe paricāretīti ||  
tam


<!-- PTS Page 344 -->
<a id="page-344"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 344]*

enam[^344-1] evam āhaṃsu ||  
Ayam ambho puriso rañño paccatthikam pasayha jīvitā voropesi ||  
tassa rājā attamano abhihāram adāsi ||  
tenāyam puriso mālī[^344-2] kuṇḍalī sunhāto suvilitto kappitakesamassu itthikāmehi[^344-3] rājāmaññe paricāretīti ||  

(II) 22 Dissati kho pana[^344-4] gāmaṇi idhekacco daḷhāya rajjuyā pacchābāhaṃ gāḷhabandhanam bandhitvā khuramuṇḍam karitvā kharassarena paṇavena rathiyāya rathiyaṃ siṅghāṭakena siṅghāṭakam pariṇetvā dakkhiṇena dvārena nikkhāmetva[^344-5] dakkhiṇato nagarassa sīsaṃ chijjamāno ||  
tam enam[^344-6] evam āhaṃsu ||  
Ambho ayam puriso kim akāsi daḷhāya rajjuyā pacchābāhaṃ gaḷhabandhanam[^344-7] bandhitvā khuramuṇḍam karitvā kharasarena paṇavena rathiyāya rathiyam siṅghātakena siṅghāṭakam parinetvā dakkhiṇena dvārena nikkhāmetvā[^344-8] dakkhiṇato nagarassa sīsam chindatīti[^344-9] ||  
tam enam[^344-10] evam āhaṃsu ||  
Ambho ayam puriso rājaverī[^344-11] itthiṃ vā purisaṃ vā jīvitā voropesi ||  
tena naṃ rājāno gahetvā evarūpaṃ kammakaraṇaṃ karontīti[^344-12] || ||  
23 Taṃ kim maññasi gāmaṇi api nu te evarūpaṃ diṭṭham vā sutaṃ vā ti || ||  
Diṭṭhañca no bhante sutañca sūyissati[^344-13] cā ti || ||  
24 Tatra gāmaṇi ye te samaṇabrahmanā evaṃvādino evaṃdiṭṭhino Yo koci pāṇam atimāpeti sabbo so diṭṭheva dhamme dukkhaṃ domanassam {paṭisaṃvediyatīti} ||  
saccaṃ vā te āhaṃsu musā vā ti || ||  
Musā bhante || ||  
Ye pana te tucchaṃ[^344-14] musā vilapanti sīlavanto vā te dussīlā vā ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 345 -->
<a id="page-345"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 345]*

Dussīlā bhante || ||  
Ye pana te dussīlā pāpadhammā micchāpaṭipannā vā te sammāpaṭipannā vā ti || ||  
Micchāpaṭipannā bhante || ||  
Ye pana te micchāpaṭipannā micchādiṭṭhikā vā te sammādiṭṭhikā vā ti || ||  
Micchādiṭṭhikā bhante || ||  
Ye pana te micchādiṭṭhikā kallaṃ nu tesu pasīditun ti || ||  
No hetam bhante || ||  

(III) 25 Dissati kho pana gāmaṇi[^345-1] idhekacco mālī[^345-2] kuṇḍalī || pe ||  
itthikāmehi rājāmaññe paricārento ||  
tam enam evam āhaṃsu ||  
Ambho ayam puriso kim akāsi māli ||  
kuṇḍalī || la ||  
itthikāmehi rājāmaññe paricāretīti || ||

Ayam ambho[^345-3] puriso rañño paccatthikassa pasayha[^345-4] ratanam[^345-5] ahāsi[^345-6] ||  
tassa rājā attamano abhihāram[^345-7] adāsi ||  
tenāyam puriso mālī kuṇḍalī || pe ||  
itthikāmehi rājāmaññe paricāretīti || ||  

(IV) 26 Dissati kho pana[^345-8] gāmaṇi idhekacco daḷhāya rajjuyā || pe ||  
dakkhiṇato nagarassa sīsam chijjamāno ||  
tam enam evam āhaṃsu || ||  
Ambho ayam puriso kim akāsi daḷhāya rajjuyā || pe ||  
dakkhiṇato nagarassa sīsaṃ chindatīti[^345-9] || ||  
Tam enam evam āhaṃsu || ||  
Ayam ambho puriso gāmā vā araññā vā adinnaṃ theyyasaṅkhātam ādiyi[^345-10] ||  
tena naṃ rājāno gahetvā evarūpaṃ kammakāraṇam[^345-11] karontīti[^345-12] || ||  
27 Taṃ kiṃ maññasi gāmaṇi api nu te evarūpaṃ diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā ti || ||  
Diṭṭhaṃ ca no bhante sutañ ca suyissati cāti[^345-13] || ||

(VII) 33 Dissati kho pana gāmaṇi idhekacco[^347-1] mālī kuṇḍalī sunhāto suvilitto kappitakesamassu itthikāmehi rājāmaññe paricārento ||

III 37 Acchariyam bhante abbhutam bhante || ||  
Atthi me bhante āvasathāgāram ||  
tattha atthi mañcakāni atthi āsanāni atthi udakamaṇiko atthi telapadīpo || ||  
Tattha[^348-1] yo samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā vāsam upeti ||  
tenāhaṃ yathāsattiṃ[^348-2] yathābalaṃ saṃvibhajāmi || ||  
Bhūtapubbam bhante cattāro satthāro nānādiṭṭhikā nānākhantikā[^348-3] nānārucikā tasmim āvasathāgāre vāsam upagacchuṃ[^348-4] ||  

(I) 38 Eko satthā evaṃvādī evaṃdiṭṭhī Natthi dinnam natthi yiṭṭhaṃ natthi hutaṃ natthi sukaṭadukkaṭānam kammānam phalaṃ vipāko ||  
natthi ayaṃ loko natthi paraloko[^348-5] natthi mātā natthi pitā natthi sattā opapātikā ||  
natthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā[^348-6] sammāpaṭipannā ye imañca lokam paraṃ ca lokaṃ sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentīti || ||  

(II) 39 Eko satthā evaṃvādī evaṃdiṭṭhī. Atthi dinnam atthi


<!-- PTS Page 349 -->
<a id="page-349"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 349]*

yiṭṭham atthi hutam atthi sukaṭadukkaṭānaṃ kammānam phalam vipāko ||  
atthi ayaṃ loko atthi paro loko atthi mātā atthi pitā atthi sattā opapātikā ||  
atthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā[^349-1] sammāpaṭipannā ye imañca lokam parañca lokam sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentīti || ||  

(III) 40 Eko satthā evaṃvādī evaṃdiṭṭhī Karato kārayato chindato chedāpayato pacato pācayato socato[^349-2] socayato[^349-3] kilamato kilamayato[^349-4] phandato phandāpayato pāṇam atimāpayato adinnam ādiyato sandhim chindato nillopaṃ harato ekāgārikam karoto paripanthe[^349-5] tiṭṭhato paradāraṃ gacchato musā bhaṇato karato[^349-6] na karīyati pāpaṃ || ||  
Khurapariyantena ce pi cakkena[^349-7] imissā pathaviyā[^349-8] pāṇe ekam maṃsakhalam ekam maṃsapuñjaṃ kareyya ||  
natthi tato nidānam pāpaṃ natthi pāpassa āgamo || ||  
Dakkhiṇañ[^349-9] ce pi Gaṅgāya tīraṃ gaccheyya hananto ghātento chindanto chedāpento pacanto pācento natthi tato nidānam pāpam natthi pāpussa āgamo || ||  
Uttarañce pi Gaṅgāya[^349-10] tīraṃ gaccheyya dadanto dāpento yajanto yajāpento[^349-11] natthi tato nidānam puññam natthi puññassa āgamo ||  
dānena damena saṃyamena saccavajjena natthi puññam natthi puññassa āgamo ti || ||  

(IV) 41 Eko satthā evaṃvādī evaṃdiṭṭhī Karato[^349-12] kārayato chindato chedāpayato pacato pācayato socato socapāyato kilamato kilamāpayato[^349-13] phandato phandāpayato pāṇam atimāpayato adinnam ādiyato sandhiṃ chidato nillopaṃ harato ekāgārikaṃ karoto paripanthe[^349-14] tiṭṭhato paradāraṃ


<!-- PTS Page 350 -->
<a id="page-350"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 350]*

gacchato musā bhaṇato karato kāriyati pāpam || ||  
Khurapariyantena ce pi cakkena[^350-1] imissā pathaviyā[^350-2] pāṇe ekam[^350-3] maṃsakhalam ekam[^350-3] maṃsapuñjaṃ kareyya ||  
atthi tato nidānam pāpam atthi pāpassa āgamo || ||  
Dakkhiṇañ ce pi Gaṅgāya[^350-4] tīram gaccheyya hananto ghātento chindanto chedāpento pacanto pācento atthi tato nidānam pāpam atthi pāpassa āgamo || ||  
Uttarañ ce pi Gaṅgāya[^350-4] tīraṃ gaccheyya dadanto dāpento yajanto yājento atthi tato nidānam puññaṃ ||  
atthi puññassa āgamo ||  
dānena damena saṃyamena saccavajjena atthi puññam atthi puññassa āgamoti || ||  
42 Tassa mayham bhante ahudeva kaṅkhā ahu[^350-5] vicikicchā ||  
Ko su nāma imesam bhavataṃ[^350-6] samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ saccam āha ko musā ti || ||  
Alaṃ hi te gāmaṇi kaṅkhitum alaṃ vicikicchituṃ ||  
kaṅkhaniye ca pana te ṭhāne vicikicchā uppannā ti || ||  
Evam pasanno ham bhante Bhagavati ||  
pahoti me Bhagavā[^350-7] tathā dhammaṃ desetuṃ yathāham imam kaṅkhādhammam pajaheyyanti || ||  

IV 43 Atthi gāmaṇi dhammasamādhi ||  
tatra ce tvaṃ cittasamādhim[^350-8] paṭilabheyyāsi evaṃ tvam imaṃ kaṅkhādhammam pajaheyyāsi ||  
katamo ca gāmaṇi dhammasamādhi || ||  

(I) 44 Idha gāmaṇi ariyasāvako pāṇātipātam pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭivirato hoti[^350-9] ||  
adinnādānam pahāya adinnādānā paṭivirato hoti ||  
kāmesu micchācāram pahāya kāmesu micchācārāpaṭivirato hoti ||  
musāvādam pahāya musāvādā


<!-- PTS Page 351 -->
<a id="page-351"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 351]*

pativirato hoti ||  
pisuṇaṃ[^351-1] vācam pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya[^351-2] paṭivirato hoti ||  
pharusaṃ[^351-3] vācam pahāya pharusāya vācāya[^351-4] paṭivirato hoti ||  
samphappalāpam pahāya samphappalāpā paṭivirato hoti ||  
abhijjham pahāya anabhijjhālu[^351-5] hoti ||  
vyāpādadosam pahāya avyāpannacitto hoti ||  
micchādiṭṭhim pahāya sammādiṭṭhiko hoti || ||  
Sa kho so gāmaṇi ariyasāvako evaṃ vigatābhijjho vigatavyāpādo asammūḷho sampajāno patissato[^351-6] mettāsahagatena cetasā ekaṃ disam pharitvā viharati ||  
tathā dutiyaṃ ||  
tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthiṃ[^351-7] ||  
iti uddham adho tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ lokam mettāsahagatena cetasā vipulena mahaggatena appamāṇena averena avyāpajjhena[^351-8] pharitvā viharati || ||  
So iti paṭisañcikkhati || ||  
Yvāyaṃ[^351-9] satthā evaṃvādī evaṃdiṭṭhī Natthi dinnaṃ natthi yiṭṭham natthi hutam natthi sukaṭadukkaṭānam phalaṃ vipāko ||  
natthi ayaṃ loko natthi paro loko natthi mātā natthi pitā natthi sattā opapātikā ||  
natthi loke samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipannā ye imañca lokam parañca lokaṃ sayam abhiññā sacchikatvā pavedentīti ||  
sa ce tassa bhoto satthuno saccaṃ vacanam apaṇṇakatāya[^351-10] mayhaṃ yo haṃ[^351-11] na kiñci vyābādhemi[^351-12] tasaṃ vā thāvaraṃ vā ubhayam ettha kaṭaggaho[^351-13] ||  
yañcamhi kāyena saṃvuto vācāya saṃvuto manasā saṃvuto ||  
yañca kāyassa bhedā param maraṇā sugatim saggaṃ lokam {upapajjissāmīti} ||  
tassa pāmujjaṃ jāyati ||  
pamuditassa pīti jāyati ||  
pītimanassa kāyo passambhati ||  
passaddhakāyo sukhaṃ vediyati[^351-14] sukhino cittaṃ samādhiyati || ||  
Ayaṃ kho gāmaṇi dhamma-


<!-- PTS Page 352 -->
<a id="page-352"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 352]*

samādhi ||  
tatra ce tvaṃ cittasamādhim paṭilabheyyāsi ||  
evaṃ tvaṃ imaṃ kaṅkhādhammam pajaheyyāsi || ||  

(II) 45 Sa kho so gāmaṇi ariyasāvako evaṃ vigatābhijjho vigatavyāpādo asammuḷho sampajāno paṭissato mettāsahagatena cetasā ekaṃ disam pharitvā viharati ||

(III) 46 Sa kho so gāmaṇi ariyasāvako evaṃ vigatābhijjho vigatavyāpādo asammuḷho sampajāno paṭissato mettāsahagatena cetasā ekaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati ||

BOOK IX- ASAṄKHATA SAṂYUTTAM

(CHAPTER I VAGGO PATHAMO)

SN_4,43(9).1 (1) Kāyo 1 Asaṅkhatañca bhikkhave desissāmi asaṅkhatagāmiñca maggaṃ ||  
taṃ sunātha || ||  
2 Katamañca bhikkhave asaṃkhataṃ Yo bhikkhave rāgakkhayo dosakkhayo mohakkhayo idaṃ vuccati bhikkhave asaṅkhataṃ || ||  
3 Katamo ca bhikkhave asaṅkhatagāmi[^359-8] maggo || ||  
Kāyagatā sati ||  
ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave asaṅkhatagāmi maggo || ||

Samatho ||

##### *[PTS Page 374]*

BOOK X AVYĀKATA-SAṂYUTTAM

SN_4,44(10).1 Khemātherī 1 Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Sāvatthiyaṃ viharati Jetavane Anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāme || ||  
2 Tena kho pana samayena Khemā bhikkhunī Kosalesu cārikam caramānā antarā ca Sāvatthiṃ[^374-1] antarā ca Sāketaṃ Toraṇavatthusmiṃ[^374-2] vāsām upagatā hoti || ||  
3 Atha kho rājā Pasenadi[^374-3] Kosalo Sāketā Sāvatthiṃ gacchanto antarā ca Sāketam antarā cā Sāvatthiṃ Toraṇavatthusmim ekarattivāsam upagacchi || ||  
4 Atha kho rājā Pasenadi Kosalo aññataram purisam āmantesi ||  
Ehi tvaṃ ambho purisa Toraṇavatthusmiṃ tathārūpaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā jāna yam aham[^374-4] ajja payirūpāseyyanti[^374-5] || ||  
Evaṃ devāti kho so puriso rañño Pasenadissa[^374-6] Kosalassa paṭissutvā kevalakappaṃ Toraṇavatthusmim[^374-7] āhiṇḍanto[^374-8] nāddasa[^374-9] tathārūpaṃ samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā yaṃ[^374-10] rājā Pasenadi Kosalo payirūpāseyya || ||  
5 Addasa[^374-11] kho so puriso Khemam bhikkhuniṃ Toraṇavatthusmiṃ vāsam upagataṃ || ||  
Disvāna yena rājā Pasenadi-Kosalo tenupasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ Pasenadi-Kosalam etad avoca || ||  
Natthi kho devā Toraṇavatthusmim tathārūpo samaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā yam devo payirupāseyya ||  
atthi ca kho deva Khemā nāma bhikkhunī tassa Bhagavato sāvikā arahato sammāsambuddhassa ||  
tassā kho pana ayyāya[^374-12] evaṃ kalyāṇo kittisaddo abbhug-

<a id="page-375"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 375]*

gato ||  
paṇḍitā viyattā medhāvinī bahussutā cittakathī[^375-1] kalyāṇapaṭibhānā ti ||[^375-2]  
taṃ devo payirūpāsatūti || ||  
6 Atha kho rājā Pasenadi-Kosalo yena Khemā bhikkhunī tenupasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Khemam bhikkhunim abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || ||

Kim panayye[^375-7] Na hoti tathagato param maraṇāti iti puṭṭhā samānā Etam pi kho mahārāja avyākatam Bhagavatā Na hoti tathāgato param maraṇāti vadesi || ||

abhinanditvā anumoditvā uṭṭhayāsanā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pakkāmīti || ||  

SN_4,44(10).2 Anurādho 1 Ekaṃ samayam Bhagavā Vesaliyaṃ viharati Mahāvane Kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ || ||  
2 Tena kho pana samayena āyasmā Anurādho[^380-1] Bhagavato avidūre araññakuṭikāyaṃ viharati || ||  
3 Atha kho sambahulā aññatitthiyā[^380-2] paribbājakā yenāyasmā Anurādho tenupasaṅkamiṃsu ||  
upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā Anurādhena[^380-3] saddhim sammodiṃsu ||  
sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu || ||  
4 Ekam antaṃ nisinnā kho te aññatitthiyā paribbājakā āyasmantam Anurādham[^380-4] etad avocuṃ || ||  
Yo so avuso Anurādha[^380-5] tathāgato uttamapuriso paramapuriso paramappattipatto taṃ tathāgato imesu catūsu[^380-6] ṭhānesu paññāpayamāno paññāpeti || ||  
Hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vā ||  
Na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vā ||  
Hoti na ca hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vā ||  
Neva hoti na na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vā || ||  
Yo so āvuso tathāgato uttamapuriso paramapuriso paramappattipatto taṃ tathāgato aññatrimehi catūhi ṭhānehi paññāpayamāno paññāpeti || ||  
Hoti tathāgato param maraṇāti vā ||  
Na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vā ||  
Hoti ca na ca hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vā ||  
Neva hoti na na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vā ti || ||  
Evaṃ vutte te[^380-7] aññatitthiyā paribbājakā āyasmantam Anurādham etad avocuṃ ||  
So[^380-8] cāyam[^380-9] bhikkhu navo bhavissati acirapabbajito thero vā pana bālo avyatto ti || ||  
5 Atha kho te aññatitthiyā paribbājakā āyasmantam Anurādham navavādena ca bālavādena ca apasādetvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkamiṃsu || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 381 -->
<a id="page-381"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 381]*

6 Atha kho āyasmato Anurādhassa acirapakkantesu aññatitthiyesu paribbājakesu[^381-1] etad ahosi || ||  
Sa ce kho maṃ te aññatitthiyā paribbājakā uttariṃ puccheyyuṃ ||  
kathaṃ vyākaramāno nu khvāhaṃ tesam aññātitthiyānam paribbājakānaṃ vuttavādī ceva Bhagavato assaṃ na ca Bhagavantam abhūtena abbhācikkheyyaṃ ||  
dhammassa cānudhammam vyākareyyam na ca koci sahadhammiko vādānupāto gārayhaṃ ṭhānam āgaccheyyāti[^381-2] || ||  
7 Atha kho āyasmā Anurādho[^381-3] yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavantam abhivādetvā ekam antaṃ nisīdi || ||  
8 Ekam antaṃ nisinno kho āyasmā Anurādho Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||  
Idhāham bhante Bhagavato avidūre araññakūṭikāyam viharāmi || ||  
Atha kho bhante sambahulā aññatitthiyā paribbājakā yenāhaṃ tenupasaṃhamiṃsu ||  
upasaṅkamitvā mayā[^381-4] saddhiṃ sammodiṃsu ||  
sammodanīyaṃ kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekam antaṃ nisīdiṃsu ||  
ekam antaṃ nisinnā kho bhante te[^381-5] aññatitthiyā paribbājakā mam etad avocuṃ ||  
Yo so āvuso[^381-5] Anurādha tathāgato uttamapuriso paramapuriso paramapattipatto taṃ tathāgato imesu catūsu ṭhānesu paññāpayamāno paññāpeti || ||  
Hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vā || pe ||  
Neva hoti na na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vā ti || ||  
Evam vutto[^381-6] ham bhante te aññatitthiye[^381-7] paribbājake etad avocaṃ ||  
Yo so avuso tathāgato uttamapuriso paramapuriso paramapattipatto taṃ tathāgato aññatrimehi catūhi ṭhānehi paññāpayamano paññāpeti ||  
Hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vā || pe ||  
Neva hoti na na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vā ti || ||  
Evaṃ vutte bhante te aññatitthiyā paribbājakā mam etad avocuṃ ||  
Yo[^381-8] cāyam bhikkhu navo bhavissati acirapabbajito thero vā pana bāloavyatto ti || ||  
Atha kho mam bhante te aññatitthiyā paribbājakā navavādena ca bālavādena ca apasādetvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkamiṃsu || ||  
9 Tassa mayham bhante acirapakkantesu tesu aññatitthi-

yesu paribbājakesu etad ahosi || ||

dhammassa cānudhammam vyākareyyaṃ ||  
na ca koci sahadhammiko vādānupāto[^382-1] gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgaccheyyāti[^382-2] || ||

evam etaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappaññāya daṭṭhabbam || ||

SN_4,44(10).9 Kutūhalasālā 2 Atha kho Vacchagotto paribbājako yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami ||

Yo[^398-5] pissa sāvako uttamapuriso paramapuriso paramapattipatto tam pi sāvakam abbhatītaṃ kālaṅkatam upapattīsu vyākaroti || Asu amutra upapanno asu amutra upapannoti ||

SN_4,44(10).10 Ānando (or Atthatto) 2 Atha kho Vacchagotto paribbājako yena Bhagavā tenupasaṅkami ||  
upasaṅkamitvā Bhagavatā saddhim sammodi ||  
sammodanīyam kathaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ vītisāretvā ekam antam nisīdi || ||

Kiṃ nu kho bho Gotama atthattā ti || ||  
Evaṃ vutte Bhagavā tuṇhī ahosi || ||  
Kim pana bho Gotamo natthattā ti || ||  
Dutiyam pi kho Bhagavā tuṇhī ahosi || ||  
Atha kho Vacchagotto paribbājako uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmi || ||  
4 Atha kho āyasmā Ānando acirapakkante Vacchagotte paribbājake Bhagavantam etad avoca || ||  
Kiṃ nu kho bhante Bhagavā Vacchagottassa paribbājakassa pañham puṭṭho na vyākāsīti || ||

7 Kiṃ nu kho bho Kaccāna Hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti iti puṭṭho samāno Avyākatam kho etaṃ Vaccha Bhagavatā Hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vadesi || ||  
8 Kim pana bho Kaccāna Na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti iti puṭṭho samāno Avyākataṃ kho etaṃ Vaccha[^402-3] Bhagavatā Na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vadesi ||  
9 Kim nu kho bho Kaccāna Hoti ca na ca[^402-4] hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti iti puṭṭho samāno Avyākatam kho etam Vaccha Bhagavatā Hoti ca na ca[^402-4] hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vadesi || ||  
10 Kim pana bho Kaccāna Neva hoti na na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti iti puṭṭho samāno Etam pi kho vaccha avyākatam Bhagavatā Neva hoti na na hoti tathāgato param maraṇā ti vadesi || ||  
11 Ko nu kho bho Kaccāna hetu ko paccayo yena tam avyākataṃ samaṇena Gotamenā ti || ||  
12 Yo ca Vaccha[^402-5] hetu yo ca paccayo[^402-6] paññāpanāya[^402-7] Rūpīti vā Arūpīti vā Saññīti vā Asaññīti vā Neva saññī nāsaññīti vā ||  
so ca hetu so ca paccayo sabbena sabbaṃ sabbathā sabbam aparisesaṃ[^402-8] nirujjheyya ||  
kena naṃ paññāpayamāno paññāpeyya Rūpīti vā Arūpīti vā Saññī vā Asaññīti va Neva saññī nāsaññīti vā ti[^402-9] || ||  
13 Kīva[^402-10] ciram pabbajito si Kaccānā ti || ||  
Na ciram āvuso tīni vassānī ti || ||  
14 Yassa passa āvuso[^402-11] ettakena ettakam eva tam passa bahuṃ[^402-12] ||  
ko pana vādo eva[^402-13] abhikkante ti || ||  


<!-- PTS Page 403 -->
<a id="page-403"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 403]*

Avyākata-saṃyuttaṃ samattam[^403-1] || ||  
Tassuddānaṃ[^403-2] || ||  
Khemā therī Anurādho || ||  
Sāriputto ti Koṭṭhiko ||  
Moggalāno ca Vaccho ca ||  
Kutuhālasālānando ||  
Sabhiyo ekādasaman ti[^403-3] || ||  
Saḷāyatana-vagga-saṃyuttaṃ-samattam[^403-4] || ||  
Tassuddānaṃ || ||  
Saḷāyatana Vedanā ||  
Mātugāmo Jambukhādako ||  
Sāmaṇḍako Moggalāno ||  
Citto Gāmaṇi Saṅkhataṃ ||  
Avyākatan ti dasadhā ti || ||  
Dasabalaselappabhavā[^403-5] nibbānamahāsamuddapariyantā aṭṭhaṅgamaggasalilā Jinavacananadī ciraṃ vahatu[^403-6] || ||  
Saḷāyatana-vaggo || ||
